  # aws_cloudformation_stack.legacy_agrovesto_infra_pipeline will be updated in-place
  # (imported from "agrovesto-infra-pipeline")
  ~ resource "aws_cloudformation_stack" "legacy_agrovesto_infra_pipeline" {
        capabilities       = [
            "CAPABILITY_NAMED_IAM",
        ]
        disable_rollback   = false
        iam_role_arn       = null
        id                 = "agrovesto-infra-pipeline"
        name               = "agrovesto-infra-pipeline"
      ~ outputs            = {} -> (known after apply)
        parameters         = {}
        tags               = {}
      ~ tags_all           = {
          + "Environment" = "prod"
          + "ManagedBy"   = "terraform"
          + "Project"     = "agrovesto"
        }
        template_body      = <<-EOT
            AWSTemplateFormatVersion: "2010-09-09"
            Parameters:
              CustomerName:
                Type: String
                Description: The name of the customer to prefix AWS resources
            
              CreateDev:
                Type: String
                AllowedValues:
                  - true
                  - false
                Default: false
                Description: Set to true if you want to create a pipeline for the dev branch
            
              CreateDr:
                Type: String
                AllowedValues:
                  - true
                  - false
                Default: false
                Description: Set to true if you want to create a pipeline for the dr branch
            
              GitHubRepoOwner:
                Type: String
                Description: The GitHub account owner of the repository
            
              GitHubRepoName:
                Type: String
                Description: The name of the GitHub repository
            
              ProdAccountID:
                Type: String
                Description: Account ID for the production environment.
            
              DevAccountID:
                Type: String
                Description: Account ID for the development environment.
            
              DRAccountID:
                Type: String
                Description: Account ID for the disaster recovery environment.
            
            Resources:
              GithubConnection:
                Type: AWS::CodeStarConnections::Connection
                Properties:
                  ConnectionName: !Sub ${CustomerName}-GithubConnection
                  ProviderType: GitHub
            
              # S3 Bucket for Pipeline Artifacts
              PipelineBucket:
                Type: AWS::S3::Bucket
                Properties:
                  BucketName: !Sub ${CustomerName}-pipeline-artifacts
            
              # IAM Role for CodePipeline
              CodePipelineRole:
                Type: AWS::IAM::Role
                Properties:
                  AssumeRolePolicyDocument:
                    Version: "2012-10-17"
                    Statement:
                      - Effect: Allow
                        Principal:
                          Service: codepipeline.amazonaws.com
                        Action: sts:AssumeRole
                  ManagedPolicyArns:
                    - arn:aws:iam::aws:policy/PowerUserAccess # Grants PowerUser access to the pipeline
            
              CodeBuildServiceRole:
                Type: AWS::IAM::Role
                Properties:
                  RoleName: CodeBuildServiceRole
                  AssumeRolePolicyDocument:
                    Version: "2012-10-17"
                    Statement:
                      - Effect: Allow
                        Principal:
                          Service: codebuild.amazonaws.com
                        Action: sts:AssumeRole
                  Policies:
                    - PolicyName: DevAssumeCodeBuildDeploymentRole
                      PolicyDocument:
                        Version: "2012-10-17"
                        Statement:
                          - Effect: Allow
                            Action: sts:AssumeRole
                            Resource: !Sub arn:aws:iam::*:role/CodeBuildDeploymentRole
                    - PolicyName: DevCodeBuildLogsPolicy
                      PolicyDocument:
                        Version: "2012-10-17"
                        Statement:
                          - Effect: Allow
                            Action:
                              - logs:DescribeLogGroups
                              - logs:GetLogEvents
                              - logs:DeleteLogGroup
                              - logs:CreateLogGroup
                              - logs:CreateLogStream
                              - logs:DeleteLogStream
                              - logs:PutLogEvents
                              - logs:DescribeLogStreams
                              - s3:GetObject
                            Resource:
                              - "arn:aws:logs:*:*:log-group:/aws/codebuild/*"
                              - "arn:aws:logs:*:*:log-group:/aws/codebuild/*:log-stream:*"
                              - "arn:aws:s3:::*"
            
              ProdCodeBuildRole:
                Type: "AWS::IAM::Role"
                Properties:
                  RoleName: "CodeBuildDeploymentRole" # Name of the role is important, EKS utilizes and potentially other services, do not change without testing and reviewing
                  AssumeRolePolicyDocument:
                    Version: "2012-10-17"
                    Statement:
                      - Effect: "Allow"
                        Principal:
                          Service:
                            - "codebuild.amazonaws.com"
                        Action:
                          - "sts:AssumeRole"
                  ManagedPolicyArns:
                    - "arn:aws:iam::aws:policy/AdministratorAccess"
            
              # Codebuild Project for Production
              ProdCodeBuildProject:
                Type: AWS::CodeBuild::Project
                Properties:
                  Name: !Sub ${CustomerName}-ProdCodeBuildProject
                  Source:
                    Type: CODEPIPELINE
                    BuildSpec: !Sub |
                      version: 0.2
                      phases:
                        install:
                          runtime-versions:
                            nodejs: 20
                          commands:
                            - echo Installing dependencies...
                            - npm install -g aws-cdk
                            - pip3 install -r requirements.txt
                            - echo Dependencies installed.
                        pre_build:
                          commands:
                            - echo Bootstrapping the environment...
                            - cdk bootstrap aws://$AWS_ACCOUNT_ID/$AWS_REGION
                            - echo Bootstrapping complete.
                        build:
                          commands:
                            - echo Synthesizing the CloudFormation template...
                            - cdk synth
                            - echo Synthesis complete.
                            - echo Deploying the CDK stack...
                            - cdk deploy --require-approval never
                      artifacts:
                        files:
                          - "**/*"
                      cache:
                        paths:
                          - node_modules/**/*
                      env:
                        variables:
                          AWS_ACCOUNT_ID: ${ProdAccountID}
                          AWS_REGION: ${AWS::Region}
                  Artifacts:
                    Type: CODEPIPELINE
                  Environment:
                    ComputeType: BUILD_GENERAL1_SMALL
                    Image: aws/codebuild/standard:5.0
                    Type: LINUX_CONTAINER
                  ServiceRole: !GetAtt ProdCodeBuildRole.Arn
            
              # Prod Pipeline
              ProdPipeline:
                Type: AWS::CodePipeline::Pipeline
                Properties:
                  Name: !Sub ${CustomerName}-ProdPipeline # Prefix customer name
                  RoleArn: !GetAtt CodePipelineRole.Arn
                  PipelineType: V2
                  ArtifactStore:
                    Type: S3
                    Location: !Ref PipelineBucket
                  Stages:
                    - Name: Source
                      Actions:
                        - Name: SourceAction
                          ActionTypeId:
                            Category: Source
                            Owner: AWS
                            Provider: CodeStarSourceConnection
                            Version: "1"
                          OutputArtifacts:
                            - Name: SourceOutput
                          Configuration:
                            ConnectionArn: !Ref GithubConnection
                            FullRepositoryId: !Sub ${GitHubRepoOwner}/${GitHubRepoName}
                            BranchName: prod
                          RunOrder: 1
                    - Name: Build
                      Actions:
                        - Name: BuildAction
                          ActionTypeId:
                            Category: Build
                            Owner: AWS
                            Provider: CodeBuild
                            Version: "1"
                          InputArtifacts:
                            - Name: SourceOutput
                          Configuration:
                            ProjectName: !Ref ProdCodeBuildProject
                          RunOrder: 1
            
              # Webhook for Prod Pipeline (triggered on push to prod)
              ProdPipelineWebhook:
                Type: AWS::CodePipeline::Webhook
                Properties:
                  Authentication: GITHUB_HMAC
                  AuthenticationConfiguration:
                    SecretToken: !Ref GithubConnection
                  Filters:
                    - JsonPath: $.ref
                      MatchEquals: refs/heads/prod # Trigger when pushing to prod
                  TargetPipeline: !Ref ProdPipeline
                  TargetAction: Source
                  Name: !Sub ${CustomerName}-ProdWebhook
                  TargetPipelineVersion: 1
            
                # CodeBuild Project for Development
              DevCodeBuildProject:
                Condition: CreateDevPipeline
                Type: AWS::CodeBuild::Project
                Properties:
                  Name: !Sub ${CustomerName}-DevCodeBuildProject
                  Source:
                    Type: CODEPIPELINE
                    BuildSpec: !Sub |
                      version: 0.2
                      phases:
                        install:
                          runtime-versions:
                            nodejs: 20
                          commands:
                            - echo Installing dependencies...
                            - npm install -g aws-cdk
                            - pip3 install -r requirements.txt
                            - echo Dependencies installed.Region
                        pre_build:
                          commands:
                            - echo Assuming the CodeBuildDeploymentRole...
                            - >
                              ROLE_SESSION_NAME=codebuild-$(date +%s)
                            - >
                              CREDS=$(aws sts assume-role --role-arn arn:aws:iam::${DevAccountID}:role/CodeBuildDeploymentRole --role-session-name $ROLE_SESSION_NAME)
                            - >
                              export AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID=$(echo $CREDS | jq -r .Credentials.AccessKeyId)
                            - >
                              export AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY=$(echo $CREDS | jq -r .Credentials.SecretAccessKey)
                            - >
                              export AWS_SESSION_TOKEN=$(echo $CREDS | jq -r .Credentials.SessionToken)
                            - echo Role assumed successfully
                            - echo Bootstrapping the environment...
                            - cdk bootstrap aws://$AWS_ACCOUNT_ID/$AWS_REGION
                            - echo Bootstrapping complete.
                        build:
                          commands:
                            - echo Synthesizing the CloudFormation template...
                            - cdk synth
                            - echo Synthesis complete.
                            - echo Deploying the CDK stack...
                            - cdk deploy --require-approval never
                      artifacts:
                        files:
                          - "**/*"
                      cache:
                        paths:
                          - node_modules/**/*
                      env:
                        variables:
                          AWS_ACCOUNT_ID: ${DevAccountID}
                          AWS_REGION: ${AWS::Region}
                  Artifacts:
                    Type: CODEPIPELINE
                  Environment:
                    ComputeType: BUILD_GENERAL1_SMALL
                    Image: aws/codebuild/standard:5.0
                    Type: LINUX_CONTAINER
                  ServiceRole: !GetAtt CodeBuildServiceRole.Arn
            
              # Dev Pipeline
              DevPipeline:
                Condition: CreateDevPipeline
                Type: AWS::CodePipeline::Pipeline
                Properties:
                  Name: !Sub ${CustomerName}-DevPipeline # Prefix customer name
                  RoleArn: !GetAtt CodePipelineRole.Arn
                  PipelineType: V2
                  ArtifactStore:
                    Type: S3
                    Location: !Ref PipelineBucket
                  Stages:
                    - Name: Source
                      Actions:
                        - Name: SourceAction
                          ActionTypeId:
                            Category: Source
                            Owner: AWS
                            Provider: CodeStarSourceConnection
                            Version: "1"
                          OutputArtifacts:
                            - Name: SourceOutput
                          Configuration:
                            ConnectionArn: !Ref GithubConnection
                            FullRepositoryId: !Sub ${GitHubRepoOwner}/${GitHubRepoName}
                            BranchName: dev
                          RunOrder: 1
                    - Name: Build
                      Actions:
                        - Name: BuildAction
                          ActionTypeId:
                            Category: Build
                            Owner: AWS
                            Provider: CodeBuild
                            Version: "1"
                          InputArtifacts:
                            - Name: SourceOutput
                          Configuration:
                            ProjectName: !Ref DevCodeBuildProject
                          RunOrder: 1
            
              # Webhook for Dev Pipeline (triggered on push to dev)
              DevPipelineWebhook:
                Condition: CreateDevPipeline
                Type: AWS::CodePipeline::Webhook
                Properties:
                  Authentication: GITHUB_HMAC
                  AuthenticationConfiguration:
                    SecretToken: !Ref GithubConnection
                  Filters:
                    - JsonPath: $.ref
                      MatchEquals: refs/heads/dev # Trigger when pushing to dev
                  TargetPipeline: !Ref DevPipeline
                  TargetAction: Source
                  Name: !Sub ${CustomerName}-DevWebhook
                  TargetPipelineVersion: 1
            
              # CodeBuild Project for Disaster Recovery (DR)
              DRCodeBuildProject:
                Condition: CreateDrPipeline
                Type: AWS::CodeBuild::Project
                Properties:
                  Name: !Sub ${CustomerName}-DRCodeBuildProject
                  Source:
                    Type: CODEPIPELINE
                    BuildSpec: !Sub |
                      version: 0.2
                      phases:
                        install:
                          runtime-versions:
                            nodejs: 20
                          commands:
                            - echo Installing dependencies...
                            - npm install -g aws-cdk
                            - pip3 install -r requirements.txt
                            - echo Dependencies installed.
                        pre_build:
                          commands:
                            - echo Assuming the CodeBuildDeploymentRole...
                            - >
                              ROLE_SESSION_NAME=codebuild-$(date +%s)
                            - >
                              CREDS=$(aws sts assume-role --role-arn arn:aws:iam::${DevAccountID}:role/CodeBuildDeploymentRole --role-session-name $ROLE_SESSION_NAME)
                            - >
                              export AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID=$(echo $CREDS | jq -r .Credentials.AccessKeyId)
                            - >
                              export AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY=$(echo $CREDS | jq -r .Credentials.SecretAccessKey)
                            - >
                              export AWS_SESSION_TOKEN=$(echo $CREDS | jq -r .Credentials.SessionToken)
                            - echo Role assumed successfully
                            - echo Bootstrapping the environment...
                            - cdk bootstrap aws://$AWS_ACCOUNT_ID/$AWS_REGION
                            - echo Bootstrapping complete.
                        build:
                          commands:
                            - echo Synthesizing the CloudFormation template...
                            - cdk synth
                            - echo Synthesis complete.
                            - echo Deploying the CDK stack...
                            - cdk deploy --require-approval never
                      artifacts:
                        files:
                          - "**/*"
                      cache:
                        paths:
                          - node_modules/**/*
                      env:
                        variables:
                          AWS_ACCOUNT_ID: ${DRAccountID}
                          AWS_REGION: ${AWS::Region}
                  Artifacts:
                    Type: CODEPIPELINE
                  Environment:
                    ComputeType: BUILD_GENERAL1_SMALL
                    Image: aws/codebuild/standard:5.0
                    Type: LINUX_CONTAINER
                  ServiceRole: !GetAtt CodeBuildServiceRole.Arn
            
              # DR Pipeline
              DrPipeline:
                Condition: CreateDrPipeline
                Type: AWS::CodePipeline::Pipeline
                Properties:
                  Name: !Sub ${CustomerName}-DrPipeline # Prefix customer name
                  RoleArn: !GetAtt CodePipelineRole.Arn
                  PipelineType: V2
                  ArtifactStore:
                    Type: S3
                    Location: !Ref PipelineBucket
                  Stages:
                    - Name: Source
                      Actions:
                        - Name: SourceAction
                          ActionTypeId:
                            Category: Source
                            Owner: AWS
                            Provider: CodeStarSourceConnection
                            Version: "1"
                          OutputArtifacts:
                            - Name: SourceOutput
                          Configuration:
                            ConnectionArn: !Ref GithubConnection
                            FullRepositoryId: !Sub ${GitHubRepoOwner}/${GitHubRepoName}
                            BranchName: dr
                          RunOrder: 1
                    - Name: Build
                      Actions:
                        - Name: BuildAction
                          ActionTypeId:
                            Category: Build
                            Owner: AWS
                            Provider: CodeBuild
                            Version: "1"
                          InputArtifacts:
                            - Name: SourceOutput
                          Configuration:
                            ProjectName: !Ref DRCodeBuildProject
                          RunOrder: 1
            
              # Webhook for DR Pipeline (triggered on push to dr)
              DrPipelineWebhook:
                Condition: CreateDrPipeline
                Type: AWS::CodePipeline::Webhook
                Properties:
                  Authentication: GITHUB_HMAC
                  AuthenticationConfiguration:
                    SecretToken: !Ref GithubConnection
                  Filters:
                    - JsonPath: $.ref
                      MatchEquals: refs/heads/dr # Trigger when pushing to dr
                  TargetPipeline: !Ref DrPipeline
                  TargetAction: Source
                  Name: !Sub ${CustomerName}-DrWebhook
                  TargetPipelineVersion: 1
            
            Conditions:
              CreateDevPipeline: !Equals
                - !Ref CreateDev
                - true
              CreateDrPipeline: !Equals
                - !Ref CreateDr
                - true
        EOT
        timeout_in_minutes = 0
    }
